import React, { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import { 
  View, 
  Text, 
  TouchableOpacity, 
  ScrollView, 
  Animated,
  StyleSheet,
  TextInput,
  Modal,
  Alert
} from 'react-native';

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// TYPE DEFINITIONS
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

type ContainerId = 'late' | 'morning' | 'afternoon' | 'evening';
type ItemType = 'grounding' | 'custom' | 'appointment';
type Outcome = 'did' | 'skipped' | 'forgot' | 'couldnt' | 'not_relevant';
type Afterfeel = 'lighter' | 'same' | 'spikier';
type ResonanceTag = 'water' | 'light' | 'movement' | 'sound' | 'novelty' | 'social';
type MissReason = 'forgot' | 'overwhelm' | 'transport' | 'conflict' | 'logistics' | 'cost' | 'not_ready';
type PressureMode = 'normal' | 'softened';

interface ContainerItem {
  id: string;
  type: ItemType;
  title: string;
  body_cue?: string;
  micro_version?: string;
  container: ContainerId;

// If appointment
  time?: string;
  date?: string;
  warmup_enabled?: boolean;
  warmup_task?: string;
  buffer_minutes?: number;
}

interface Completion {
  item_id: string;
  container: ContainerId;
  date: string;
  outcome: Outcome;
  afterfeel?: Afterfeel;
  resonance_tags?: ResonanceTag[];
  miss_reason?: MissReason;
}

interface TimeContainer {
  id: ContainerId;
  label: string;
  window: [number, number];
  pressure_mode: PressureMode;
}

interface ThemeColors {
  bg: string;
  card: string;
  accent: string;
  text: string;
  dim: string;
}

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

---

// DEFAULT GROUNDING TASKS BY CONTAINER
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

const DEFAULT_GROUNDING: { [key in ContainerId]: Omit<ContainerItem, 'id' | 'container'>[] } = {
  late: [
    { type: 'grounding', title: 'three slow breaths', body_cue: 'inhale 4, hold 2, exhale 6', micro_version: 'one breath' },
    { type: 'grounding', title: 'body scan lying down', body_cue: 'notice feet, legs, belly, chest', micro_version: 'just notice feet' },
    { type: 'grounding', title: 'sip water slowly', body_cue: 'three slow swallows, notice temperature', micro_version: 'one sip' },
  ],
  morning: [
    { type: 'grounding', title: 'water face', body_cue: 'cold water, three slow handfuls', micro_version: 'splash + towel press 20s' },
    { type: 'grounding', title: 'open window 10s', body_cue: 'three full breaths while light touches face', micro_version: 'crack window + 2 breaths' },
    { type: 'grounding', title: 'sip water', body_cue: 'slow swallows, notice temperature', micro_version: 'one sip' },
    { type: 'grounding', title: 'stretch gently', body_cue: 'arms up, side bend, breathe', micro_version: 'arms up + one breath' },
  ],
  afternoon: [
    { type: 'grounding', title: 'stand + sigh twice', body_cue: 'big inhale, longer exhale with sound', micro_version: 'one sigh' },
    { type: 'grounding', title: 'notice 3 blue things', body_cue: 'soften gaze, peripheral vision', micro_version: 'one blue thing' },
    { type: 'grounding', title: 'stretch neck', body_cue: 'slow head rolls, pause where tight', micro_version: 'two gentle tilts' },
    { type: 'grounding', title: 'wipe phone screen', body_cue: 'gentle circles, like petting glass', micro_version: '10s wipe' },
  ],
  evening: [
    { type: 'grounding', title: 'dim lights', body_cue: 'notice the shift in atmosphere', micro_version: 'one lamp off' },
    { type: 'grounding', title: 'change to soft clothes', body_cue: 'notice texture, let fabric hug you', micro_version: 'remove one tight thing' },
    { type: 'grounding', title: 'lie on floor 2min', body_cue: 'let gravity hold you, scan body', micro_version: 'lie down 30s' },
    { type: 'grounding', title: 'journal 2 sentences', body_cue: 'let hand move without thinking', micro_version: 'one word ok' },
  ]
};

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// THEMES
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

const THEMES: { [key: string]: ThemeColors } = {
  forest: {
    bg: '#1a2f23',
    card: '#2d4a3a',
    accent: '#6b9e78',
    text: '#e8f4ea',
    dim: '#5a7a64'
  },
  studio: {
    bg: '#f5f5f0',
    card: '#ffffff',
    accent: '#4a5568',
    text: '#2d3748',
    dim: '#a0aec0'
  },
  cosmic: {
    bg: '#0f0f23',
    card: '#1a1a3e',
    accent: '#7c3aed',
    text: '#e9d5ff',
    dim: '#6b46c1'
  }
};

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// UTILITY: GET CURRENT CONTAINER
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

const getCurrentContainer = (): ContainerId => {
  const hour = new Date().getHours();
  if (hour >= 0 && hour < 6) return 'late';
  if (hour >= 6 && hour < 12) return 'morning';
  if (hour >= 12 && hour < 18) return 'afternoon';
  return 'evening';
};

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// MAIN COMPONENT
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

export default function PDATaskAppV2() {
  // Theme
  const [theme, setTheme] = useState('forest');
  const colors = THEMES[theme];

// Navigation
  const [currentScreen, setCurrentScreen] = useState<'containers' | 'item-detail' | 'outcome' | 'add-item' | 'patterns'>('containers');

// Time containers
  const [containers] = useState<TimeContainer[]>([
    { id: 'late', label: 'Late Night / Early Morning', window: [0, 6], pressure_mode: 'normal' },
    { id: 'morning', label: 'Morning', window: [6, 12], pressure_mode: 'normal' },
    { id: 'afternoon', label: 'Afternoon', window: [12, 18], pressure_mode: 'normal' },
    { id: 'evening', label: 'Evening', window: [18, 24], pressure_mode: 'normal' }
  ]);

// Items (grounding + custom + appointments)
  const [items, setItems] = useState<ContainerItem[]>(() => {
    // Initialize with default grounding tasks
    const allItems: ContainerItem[] = [];
    let idCounter = 0;

Object.entries(DEFAULT_GROUNDING).forEach(([containerId, tasks]) => {
      tasks.forEach(task => {
        allItems.push({
          ...task,
          id: `item-${idCounter++}`,
          container: containerId as ContainerId
        });
      });
    });

return allItems;
  });

// Completions history
  const [completions, setCompletions] = useState<Completion[]>([]);

// Completion phrases (rotates, never repeats consecutively)
  const [lastPhraseIndex, setLastPhraseIndex] = useState(-1);
  const completionPhrases = [
    'good contact.',
    'small shift made.',
    'system heard you.',
    'thread tugged.',
    'momentum felt.',

---

'anchor touched.'
  ];

const getCompletionPhrase = (): string => {
    let newIndex;
    do {
      newIndex = Math.floor(Math.random() * completionPhrases.length);
    } while (newIndex === lastPhraseIndex && completionPhrases.length > 1);
    setLastPhraseIndex(newIndex);
    return completionPhrases[newIndex];
  };

// Current state
  const [currentContainer, setCurrentContainer] = useState<ContainerId>(getCurrentContainer());
  const [activeItem, setActiveItem] = useState<ContainerItem | null>(null);
  const [showFirstPixel, setShowFirstPixel] = useState(true);
  const [selectedOutcome, setSelectedOutcome] = useState<Outcome | null>(null);
  const [showShiftModal, setShowShiftModal] = useState(false);
  const [shiftYesCount, setShiftYesCount] = useState(0);
  const [completionPhrase, setCompletionPhrase] = useState('');

// Outcome screen state (must be at top level, not in conditional render)
  const [tempAfterfeel, setTempAfterfeel] = useState<Afterfeel | null>(null);
  const [tempTags, setTempTags] = useState<ResonanceTag[]>([]);

// Add item modal
  const [addModalVisible, setAddModalVisible] = useState(false);
  const [newItemTitle, setNewItemTitle] = useState('');
  const [newItemType, setNewItemType] = useState<'custom' | 'appointment'>('custom');
  const [newItemTime, setNewItemTime] = useState('');

// Animation
  const [fadeAnim] = useState(new Animated.Value(1));
  const [pulseAnim] = useState(new Animated.Value(1));

// Update current container every minute
  useEffect(() => {
    const interval = setInterval(() => {
      setCurrentContainer(getCurrentContainer());
    }, 60000);
    return () => clearInterval(interval);
  }, []);

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
  // ANIMATION HELPERS
  // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

const triggerExhaleAnimation = () => {
    // Randomize amplitude ±5% for organic feel
    const amplitude = 0.98 + (Math.random() * 0.02 - 0.01);

pulseAnim.setValue(1);
    Animated.sequence([
      Animated.timing(pulseAnim, {
        toValue: amplitude,
        duration: 200,
        useNativeDriver: true
      }),
      Animated.timing(pulseAnim, {
        toValue: 1,
        duration: 200,
        useNativeDriver: true
      })
    ]).start();
  };

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
  // HELPERS
  // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

const getContainerItems = (containerId: ContainerId): ContainerItem[] => {
    return items.filter(item => item.container === containerId);
  };

const getTodayCompletions = (): Completion[] => {
    const today = new Date().toISOString().split('T')[0];
    return completions.filter(c => c.date === today);
  };

const getItemCompletion = (itemId: string): Completion | undefined => {
    const today = new Date().toISOString().split('T')[0];
    return completions.find(c => c.item_id === itemId && c.date === today);
  };

const handleItemTap = (item: ContainerItem) => {
    setActiveItem(item);
    setShowFirstPixel(false);
    setCurrentScreen('item-detail');
    fadeAnim.setValue(0);
    Animated.timing(fadeAnim, { toValue: 1, duration: 300, useNativeDriver: true }).start();
  };

const handleDeleteItem = (itemId: string) => {
    const item = items.find(i => i.id === itemId);
    if (item && item.type !== 'grounding') {
      Alert.alert(
        'Delete ritual?',
        `Remove "${item.title}" from your rituals?`,
        [
          { text: 'Cancel', style: 'cancel' },
          { 
            text: 'Delete', 
            style: 'destructive',
            onPress: () => {
              setItems(items.filter(i => i.id !== itemId));
              // Also remove any completions for this item
              setCompletions(completions.filter(c => c.item_id !== itemId));
            }
          }
        ]
      );
    }
  };

const handleOutcomeSelect = (outcome: Outcome) => {
    if (!activeItem) return;

// Trigger exhale animation
    triggerExhaleAnimation();

if (outcome === 'did') {
      setSelectedOutcome(outcome);
      // Show shift modal if user hasn't disabled it
      if (shiftYesCount < 3) {
        setShowShiftModal(true);
      } else {
        // Evolved to micro-affirmation
        setCompletionPhrase('small current moved');
        setTimeout(() => {
          setTempAfterfeel(null);
          setTempTags([]);
          setCurrentScreen('outcome');
          setCompletionPhrase('');
        }, 1500);
      }
    } else {
      // For non-did outcomes, save directly
      const completion: Completion = {
        item_id: activeItem.id,
        container: activeItem.container,
        date: new Date().toISOString().split('T')[0],
        outcome
      };
      setCompletions([...completions, completion]);
      setCompletionPhrase(getCompletionPhrase());

// Show phrase briefly then return
      setTimeout(() => {
        setActiveItem(null);
        setCurrentScreen('containers');
        setCompletionPhrase('');
      }, 1200);
    }
  };

const handleAfterfeelSelect = (afterfeel: Afterfeel, tags: ResonanceTag[]) => {
    if (!activeItem || !selectedOutcome) return;

const completion: Completion = {
      item_id: activeItem.id,
      container: activeItem.container,
      date: new Date().toISOString().split('T')[0],
      outcome: selectedOutcome,
      afterfeel,
      resonance_tags: tags
    };

setCompletions([...completions, completion]);
    setCompletionPhrase(getCompletionPhrase());

// Show phrase briefly
    setTimeout(() => {
      setActiveItem(null);
      setSelectedOutcome(null);
      setCurrentScreen('containers');
      setCompletionPhrase('');
    }, 1200);
  };

const handleShiftResponse = (felt: boolean) => {
    if (felt) {
      setShiftYesCount(shiftYesCount + 1);
    }
    setShowShiftModal(false);
    // Reset outcome state when entering screen
    setTempAfterfeel(null);
    setTempTags([]);
    setCurrentScreen('outcome');
  };

const handleShiftSkipAlways = () => {
    setShiftYesCount(999); // Effectively disables it
    setShowShiftModal(false);
    // Reset outcome state when entering screen
    setTempAfterfeel(null);
    setTempTags([]);
    setCurrentScreen('outcome');
  };

const handleAddItem = () => {
    if (!newItemTitle.trim()) return;

const newItem: ContainerItem = {
      id: `item-${Date.now()}`,
      type: newItemType,
      title: newItemTitle,
      container: currentContainer,
      time: newItemType === 'appointment' ? newItemTime : undefined,
      date: newItemType === 'appointment' ? new Date().toISOString().split('T')[0] : undefined

---

};

setItems([...items, newItem]);
    setNewItemTitle('');
    setNewItemTime('');
    setAddModalVisible(false);
  };

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
  // PATTERN INSIGHTS
  // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

const getPatternInsights = () => {
    const today = new Date();
    const weekAgo = new Date(today.getTime() - 7 * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000);
    const weekCompletions = completions.filter(c => new Date(c.date) >= weekAgo);

// Calculate completion rates per item
    const itemStats: { [key: string]: { did: number; total: number; tags: ResonanceTag[] } } = {};

items.forEach(item => {
      const itemCompletions = weekCompletions.filter(c => c.item_id === item.id);
      const didCount = itemCompletions.filter(c => c.outcome === 'did').length;
      const allTags = itemCompletions.flatMap(c => c.resonance_tags || []);

itemStats[item.id] = {
        did: didCount,
        total: itemCompletions.length,
        tags: allTags
      };
    });

// Find resonating tasks (high completion)
    const resonating = items
      .filter(item => itemStats[item.id]?.did >= 4)
      .map(item => ({ item, count: itemStats[item.id].did }));

// Find not-sticking tasks (low completion)
    const notSticking = items
      .filter(item => itemStats[item.id]?.total >= 3 && itemStats[item.id]?.did <= 1)
      .map(item => ({ item, count: itemStats[item.id].did }));

return { resonating, notSticking, weekCompletions };
  };

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
  // RENDER: CONTAINERS VIEW (Main Screen)
  // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

if (currentScreen === 'containers') {
    const containerData = containers.find(c => c.id === currentContainer);
    const containerItems = getContainerItems(currentContainer);

// First pixel: pick one incomplete item
    const incompleteItems = containerItems.filter(item => !getItemCompletion(item.id));
    const firstPixelItem = incompleteItems.length > 0 ? incompleteItems[0] : null;

return (
      <View style={[styles.container, { backgroundColor: colors.bg }]}>
        <ScrollView contentContainerStyle={styles.scrollContent}>
          {/* Header */}
          <View style={styles.header}>
            <Text style={[styles.containerTitle, { color: colors.text }]}>
              {containerData?.label}
            </Text>
            <TouchableOpacity onPress={() => setCurrentScreen('patterns')}>
              <Text style={[styles.headerLink, { color: colors.accent }]}>patterns</Text>
            </TouchableOpacity>
          </View>

<Text style={[styles.containerSubtitle, { color: colors.dim }]}>
            {new Date().toLocaleTimeString('en-US', { hour: 'numeric', minute: '2-digit' })}
          </Text>

{/* First Pixel Win */}
          {showFirstPixel && firstPixelItem && (
            <View style={styles.firstPixelContainer}>
              <Text style={[styles.firstPixelPrompt, { color: colors.dim }]}>
                Want to try one tiny thing?
              </Text>

<TouchableOpacity
                onPress={() => handleItemTap(firstPixelItem)}
                style={[styles.firstPixelCard, { backgroundColor: colors.card, borderColor: colors.accent }]}
              >
                <Text style={[styles.itemTitle, { color: colors.text }]}>
                  {firstPixelItem.title}
                </Text>
                {firstPixelItem.body_cue && (
                  <Text style={[styles.itemCue, { color: colors.dim }]}>
                    {firstPixelItem.body_cue}
                  </Text>
                )}
                <View style={styles.itemActions}>
                  <Text style={[styles.actionText, { color: colors.accent }]}>try it</Text>
                </View>
              </TouchableOpacity>

<TouchableOpacity onPress={() => setShowFirstPixel(false)}>
                <Text style={[styles.showAllText, { color: colors.dim }]}>
                  show all →
                </Text>
              </TouchableOpacity>
            </View>
          )}

{/* All Items (visible after first pixel or if first pixel skipped) */}
          {!showFirstPixel && (
            <>
              {/* Return points section */}
              <View style={styles.section}>
                <Text style={[styles.sectionTitle, { color: colors.dim }]}>
                  RETURN POINTS
                </Text>
                {containerItems.filter(item => item.type === 'grounding').map(item => {
                  const completion = getItemCompletion(item.id);
                  return (
                    <TouchableOpacity
                      key={item.id}
                      onPress={() => handleItemTap(item)}
                      style={[
                        styles.itemCard,
                        { backgroundColor: colors.card, borderColor: completion ? colors.accent : colors.dim }
                      ]}
                    >
                      <Text style={[styles.itemTitle, { color: colors.text }]}>
                        {completion ? '✓ ' : '○ '}{item.title}
                      </Text>
                      {completion && (
                        <Text style={[styles.completionNote, { color: colors.dim }]}>
                          {completion.outcome === 'did' ? 'done' : completion.outcome}
                        </Text>
                      )}
                    </TouchableOpacity>
                  );
                })}
              </View>

{/* Your rituals section */}
              {containerItems.filter(item => item.type === 'custom').length > 0 && (
                <View style={styles.section}>
                  <Text style={[styles.sectionTitle, { color: colors.dim }]}>
                    YOUR RITUALS
                  </Text>
                  {containerItems.filter(item => item.type === 'custom').map(item => {
                    const completion = getItemCompletion(item.id);
                    return (
                      <TouchableOpacity
                        key={item.id}
                        onPress={() => handleItemTap(item)}
                        onLongPress={() => handleDeleteItem(item.id)}
                        style={[
                          styles.itemCard,
                          { backgroundColor: colors.card, borderColor: completion ? colors.accent : colors.dim }
                        ]}
                      >
                        <Text style={[styles.itemTitle, { color: colors.text }]}>
                          {completion ? '✓ ' : '○ '}{item.title}
                        </Text>
                        <Text style={[styles.longPressHint, { color: colors.dim }]}>
                          (long press to delete)
                        </Text>
                      </TouchableOpacity>
                    );
                  })}
                </View>
              )}

{/* Time markers section */}
              {containerItems.filter(item => item.type === 'appointment').length > 0 && (
                <View style={styles.section}>
                  <Text style={[styles.sectionTitle, { color: colors.dim }]}>
                    TIME MARKERS
                  </Text>
                  {containerItems.filter(item => item.type === 'appointment').map(item => {
                    const completion = getItemCompletion(item.id);
                    return (
                      <TouchableOpacity
                        key={item.id}
                        onPress={() => handleItemTap(item)}
                        onLongPress={() => handleDeleteItem(item.id)}
                        style={[
                          styles.itemCard,
                          { backgroundColor: colors.card, borderColor: completion ? colors.accent : colors.dim }
                        ]}
                      >

---

<Text style={[styles.itemTitle, { color: colors.text }]}>
                          {item.time && `${item.time} • `}{item.title}
                        </Text>
                        {completion && (
                          <Text style={[styles.completionNote, { color: colors.dim }]}>
                            {completion.outcome === 'did' ? 'went' : completion.outcome}
                          </Text>
                        )}
                        <Text style={[styles.longPressHint, { color: colors.dim }]}>
                          (long press to delete)
                        </Text>
                      </TouchableOpacity>
                    );
                  })}
                </View>
              )}

{/* Craft moment button */}
              <TouchableOpacity
                onPress={() => setAddModalVisible(true)}
                style={[styles.addButton, { backgroundColor: colors.card, borderColor: colors.accent }]}
              >
                <Text style={[styles.addButtonText, { color: colors.accent }]}>
                  + craft your moment
                </Text>
              </TouchableOpacity>
            </>
          )}

{/* Container navigation */}
          <View style={styles.containerNav}>
            {containers.map(c => (
              <TouchableOpacity
                key={c.id}
                onPress={() => {
                  setCurrentContainer(c.id);
                  setShowFirstPixel(true);
                }}
                style={[
                  styles.containerNavButton,
                  { 
                    backgroundColor: c.id === currentContainer ? colors.accent : colors.card,
                    borderColor: colors.accent
                  }
                ]}
              >
                <Text style={[styles.containerNavText, { color: c.id === currentContainer ? colors.text : colors.dim }]}>
                  {c.label.split(' ')[0]}
                </Text>
              </TouchableOpacity>
            ))}
          </View>

{/* Theme switcher */}
          <View style={styles.themeSwitcher}>
            {Object.keys(THEMES).map(t => (
              <TouchableOpacity
                key={t}
                onPress={() => setTheme(t)}
                style={[
                  styles.themeCircle,
                  { 
                    backgroundColor: THEMES[t].accent,
                    borderWidth: theme === t ? 3 : 0,
                    borderColor: colors.text
                  }
                ]}
              />
            ))}
          </View>
        </ScrollView>

{/* Add Item Modal */}
        <Modal visible={addModalVisible} transparent animationType="slide">
          <View style={styles.modalOverlay}>
            <View style={[styles.modalContent, { backgroundColor: colors.card }]}>
              <Text style={[styles.modalTitle, { color: colors.text }]}>Add to {containerData?.label}</Text>

<View style={styles.typeSelector}>
                <TouchableOpacity
                  onPress={() => setNewItemType('custom')}
                  style={[
                    styles.typeButton,
                    { 
                      backgroundColor: newItemType === 'custom' ? colors.accent : colors.bg,
                      borderColor: colors.accent
                    }
                  ]}
                >
                  <Text style={[styles.typeButtonText, { color: colors.text }]}>Ritual</Text>
                </TouchableOpacity>
                <TouchableOpacity
                  onPress={() => setNewItemType('appointment')}
                  style={[
                    styles.typeButton,
                    { 
                      backgroundColor: newItemType === 'appointment' ? colors.accent : colors.bg,
                      borderColor: colors.accent
                    }
                  ]}
                >
                  <Text style={[styles.typeButtonText, { color: colors.text }]}>Time Marker</Text>
                </TouchableOpacity>
              </View>

<TextInput
                style={[styles.input, { backgroundColor: colors.bg, color: colors.text, borderColor: colors.accent }]}
                placeholder="What?"
                placeholderTextColor={colors.dim}
                value={newItemTitle}
                onChangeText={setNewItemTitle}
              />

{newItemType === 'appointment' && (
                <TextInput
                  style={[styles.input, { backgroundColor: colors.bg, color: colors.text, borderColor: colors.accent }]}
                  placeholder="Time (e.g., 2:30pm)"
                  placeholderTextColor={colors.dim}
                  value={newItemTime}
                  onChangeText={setNewItemTime}
                />
              )}

<View style={styles.modalActions}>
                <TouchableOpacity
                  onPress={() => {
                    setAddModalVisible(false);
                    setNewItemTitle('');
                    setNewItemTime('');
                  }}
                  style={[styles.modalButton, { backgroundColor: colors.bg }]}
                >
                  <Text style={[styles.modalButtonText, { color: colors.dim }]}>Cancel</Text>
                </TouchableOpacity>
                <TouchableOpacity
                  onPress={handleAddItem}
                  style={[styles.modalButton, { backgroundColor: colors.accent }]}
                >
                  <Text style={[styles.modalButtonText, { color: colors.text }]}>Add</Text>
                </TouchableOpacity>
              </View>
            </View>
          </View>
        </Modal>

{/* Completion phrase overlay */}
        {completionPhrase && (
          <View style={styles.phraseOverlay}>
            <View style={[styles.phraseBox, { backgroundColor: colors.card }]}>
              <Text style={[styles.phraseText, { color: colors.accent }]}>
                {completionPhrase}
              </Text>
            </View>
          </View>
        )}

{/* Shift modal */}
        <Modal visible={showShiftModal} transparent animationType="fade">
          <View style={styles.shiftModalOverlay}>
            <View style={[styles.shiftModalContent, { backgroundColor: colors.card }]}>
              <Text style={[styles.shiftModalText, { color: colors.text }]}>
                feel that shift?
              </Text>
              <View style={styles.shiftModalButtons}>
                <TouchableOpacity
                  onPress={() => handleShiftResponse(true)}
                  style={[styles.shiftModalButton, { backgroundColor: colors.accent }]}
                >
                  <Text style={[styles.shiftModalButtonText, { color: colors.text }]}>yes</Text>
                </TouchableOpacity>
                <TouchableOpacity
                  onPress={() => handleShiftResponse(false)}
                  style={[styles.shiftModalButton, { backgroundColor: colors.card, borderColor: colors.accent, borderWidth: 1 }]}
                >
                  <Text style={[styles.shiftModalButtonText, { color: colors.text }]}>not yet</Text>
                </TouchableOpacity>
              </View>
              <TouchableOpacity onPress={handleShiftSkipAlways}>
                <Text style={[styles.shiftModalSkip, { color: colors.dim }]}>
                  skip always
                </Text>
              </TouchableOpacity>
            </View>
          </View>
        </Modal>
      </View>
    );
  }

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
  // RENDER: ITEM DETAIL
  // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

if (currentScreen === 'item-detail' && activeItem) {
    const completion = getItemCompletion(activeItem.id);

return (
      <View style={[styles.container, { backgroundColor: colors.bg }]}>
        <Animated.ScrollView 
          style={{ opacity: fadeAnim }} 
          contentContainerStyle={styles.scrollContent}
          keyboardShouldPersistTaps="handled"
          scrollEnabled={true}
        >
          <TouchableOpacity
            onPress={() => {
              setCurrentScreen('containers');
              setActiveItem(null);
            }}
            style={styles.backButton}
          >
            <Text style={[styles.backText, { color: colors.dim }]}>← back</Text>
          </TouchableOpacity>

<Animated.View style={{ transform: [{ scale: pulseAnim }] }}>
            <Text style={[styles.detailTitle, { color: colors.text }]}>
              {activeItem.title}
            </Text>
          </Animated.View>

{activeItem.body_cue && (
            <View style={[styles.cueBox, { backgroundColor: colors.card, borderLeftColor: colors.accent }]}>
              <Text style={[styles.cueLabel, { color: colors.dim }]}>body cue:</Text>
              <Text style={[styles.cueText, { color: colors.text }]}>{activeItem.body_cue}</Text>
            </View>
          )}

{activeItem.micro_version && (
            <View style={[styles.microBox, { backgroundColor: colors.card }]}>
              <Text style={[styles.microLabel, { color: colors.dim }]}>or try micro version:</Text>
              <Text style={[styles.microText, { color: colors.accent }]}>{activeItem.micro_version}</Text>
            </View>
          )}

{!completion && (
            <View style={styles.outcomeButtons} pointerEvents="box-none">
              <TouchableOpacity
                activeOpacity={0.7}
                onPress={() => handleOutcomeSelect('did')}
                style={[styles.outcomeButton, { backgroundColor: colors.accent }]}
                accessible={true}
                accessibilityLabel="Mark as did it"
              >
                <Text style={[styles.outcomeButtonText, { color: colors.text }]}>did it</Text>
              </TouchableOpacity>

<View style={styles.outcomeRow}>
                <TouchableOpacity
                  activeOpacity={0.7}
                  onPress={() => handleOutcomeSelect('skipped')}
                  style={[styles.outcomeButtonSmall, { backgroundColor: colors.card, borderColor: colors.accent }]}

---

>
                  <Text style={[styles.outcomeButtonTextSmall, { color: colors.text }]}>skipped</Text>
                </TouchableOpacity>

<TouchableOpacity
                  activeOpacity={0.7}
                  onPress={() => handleOutcomeSelect('forgot')}
                  style={[styles.outcomeButtonSmall, { backgroundColor: colors.card, borderColor: colors.accent }]}
                >
                  <Text style={[styles.outcomeButtonTextSmall, { color: colors.text }]}>forgot</Text>
                </TouchableOpacity>
              </View>

<View style={styles.outcomeRow}>
                <TouchableOpacity
                  activeOpacity={0.7}
                  onPress={() => handleOutcomeSelect('couldnt')}
                  style={[styles.outcomeButtonSmall, { backgroundColor: colors.card, borderColor: colors.accent }]}
                >
                  <Text style={[styles.outcomeButtonTextSmall, { color: colors.text }]}>couldn't</Text>
                </TouchableOpacity>

<TouchableOpacity
                  activeOpacity={0.7}
                  onPress={() => handleOutcomeSelect('not_relevant')}
                  style={[styles.outcomeButtonSmall, { backgroundColor: colors.card, borderColor: colors.dim }]}
                >
                  <Text style={[styles.outcomeButtonTextSmall, { color: colors.dim }]}>not relevant</Text>
                </TouchableOpacity>
              </View>
            </View>
          )}

{completion && (
            <View style={[styles.completedBox, { backgroundColor: colors.card }]}>
              <Text style={[styles.completedText, { color: colors.accent }]}>
                marked as: {completion.outcome}
              </Text>
              {completion.afterfeel && (
                <Text style={[styles.completedDetail, { color: colors.dim }]}>
                  felt: {completion.afterfeel}
                </Text>
              )}
              {completion.resonance_tags && completion.resonance_tags.length > 0 && (
                <Text style={[styles.completedDetail, { color: colors.dim }]}>
                  tags: {completion.resonance_tags.join(', ')}
                </Text>
              )}
            </View>
          )}
        </Animated.ScrollView>
      </View>
    );
  }

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
  // RENDER: OUTCOME (Afterfeel + Tags)
  // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

if (currentScreen === 'outcome') {
    return (
      <View style={[styles.container, { backgroundColor: colors.bg }]}>
        <ScrollView contentContainerStyle={styles.scrollContent}>
          <Text style={[styles.outcomeTitle, { color: colors.text }]}>
            How did it feel?
          </Text>

<View style={styles.afterfeelButtons}>
            {(['lighter', 'same', 'spikier'] as Afterfeel[]).map(feel => (
              <TouchableOpacity
                key={feel}
                onPress={() => setTempAfterfeel(feel)}
                style={[
                  styles.afterfeelButton,
                  { 
                    backgroundColor: tempAfterfeel === feel ? colors.accent : colors.card,
                    borderColor: colors.accent
                  }
                ]}
              >
                <Text style={[styles.afterfeelText, { color: colors.text }]}>{feel}</Text>
              </TouchableOpacity>
            ))}
          </View>

<Text style={[styles.tagsPrompt, { color: colors.dim }]}>
            What helped? (pick up to 2, or skip)
          </Text>

<View style={styles.tagsContainer}>
            {(['water', 'light', 'movement', 'sound', 'novelty', 'social'] as ResonanceTag[]).map(tag => (
              <TouchableOpacity
                key={tag}
                onPress={() => {
                  if (tempTags.includes(tag)) {
                    setTempTags(tempTags.filter(t => t !== tag));
                  } else if (tempTags.length < 2) {
                    setTempTags([...tempTags, tag]);
                  }
                }}
                style={[
                  styles.tagChip,
                  { 
                    backgroundColor: tempTags.includes(tag) ? colors.accent : colors.card,
                    borderColor: colors.accent
                  }
                ]}
              >
                <Text style={[styles.tagText, { color: colors.text }]}>{tag}</Text>
              </TouchableOpacity>
            ))}
          </View>

<TouchableOpacity
            onPress={() => {
              if (tempAfterfeel) {
                handleAfterfeelSelect(tempAfterfeel, tempTags);
              }
            }}
            style={[styles.doneButton, { backgroundColor: colors.accent, opacity: tempAfterfeel ? 1 : 0.5 }]}
            disabled={!tempAfterfeel}
          >
            <Text style={[styles.doneButtonText, { color: colors.text }]}>done</Text>
          </TouchableOpacity>

<TouchableOpacity
            onPress={() => handleAfterfeelSelect('same', [])}
          >
            <Text style={[styles.skipText, { color: colors.dim }]}>skip</Text>
          </TouchableOpacity>
        </ScrollView>
      </View>
    );
  }

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
  // RENDER: PATTERNS
  // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

if (currentScreen === 'patterns') {
    const { resonating, notSticking } = getPatternInsights();

return (
      <View style={[styles.container, { backgroundColor: colors.bg }]}>
        <ScrollView contentContainerStyle={styles.scrollContent}>
          <TouchableOpacity
            onPress={() => setCurrentScreen('containers')}
            style={styles.backButton}
          >
            <Text style={[styles.backText, { color: colors.dim }]}>← back</Text>
          </TouchableOpacity>

<Text style={[styles.patternsTitle, { color: colors.text }]}>
            This Week's Patterns
          </Text>

<Text style={[styles.patternsSubtitle, { color: colors.dim }]}>
            no judgment, just data
          </Text>

{resonating.length > 0 && (
            <View style={styles.patternSection}>
              <Text style={[styles.patternSectionTitle, { color: colors.accent }]}>
                WHAT'S WORKING
              </Text>
              {resonating.map(({ item, count }) => (
                <View key={item.id} style={[styles.patternCard, { backgroundColor: colors.card }]}>
                  <Text style={[styles.patternItemTitle, { color: colors.text }]}>
                    {item.title}
                  </Text>
                  <Text style={[styles.patternMetric, { color: colors.dim }]}>
                    {count}/7 days
                  </Text>
                </View>
              ))}
            </View>
          )}

{notSticking.length > 0 && (
            <View style={styles.patternSection}>
              <Text style={[styles.patternSectionTitle, { color: colors.dim }]}>
                NOT STICKING
              </Text>
              {notSticking.map(({ item, count }) => (
                <View key={item.id} style={[styles.patternCard, { backgroundColor: colors.card }]}>
                  <Text style={[styles.patternItemTitle, { color: colors.text }]}>
                    {item.title}
                  </Text>
                  <Text style={[styles.patternMetric, { color: colors.dim }]}>
                    {count}/7 days — maybe too much friction?
                  </Text>
                </View>
              ))}
            </View>
          )}

{resonating.length === 0 && notSticking.length === 0 && (
            <View style={[styles.emptyState, { backgroundColor: colors.card }]}>
              <Text style={[styles.emptyText, { color: colors.dim }]}>
                Not enough data yet. Check back after a few days of tracking.
              </Text>
            </View>
          )}
        </ScrollView>
      </View>
    );
  }

return null;
}

[1761100698.788057] Assistant: this is tight. you’ve captured the *shape*—containers, consentful tracking, pattern mirrors. a few refinements to make it gentler, smarter, and harder to derail:

# immediate tweaks (highest leverage)

## 1) four outcomes → five (to protect authenticity)
add **“not relevant”** for when a task shows up but the context changed (travel, guest over, water shutoff). this prevents false “skips” that pollute patterns.

```
outcome: 'did' | 'skipped' | 'forgot' | 'couldnt' | 'not_relevant'
```

## 2) first pixel win
in each container, surface **one 10-second invite** at the top (auto-chosen by context & history). one tap = momentum. everything else collapses under a chevron until that first win or an explicit “show all.”

## 3) pressure governor (auto-soften)
when `forgot + couldnt` exceed a threshold in the current container (or user flags “spiky”), auto-shrink the UI:
- show only 2 items (one grounding, one “my thing”)
- switch copy to feather tone
- silence notifications for that container
- offer “zero-demand mode today?” chip

## 4) appointment warm-ups
for scheduled items, prepend a **30–60s pre-ritual** suggestion one hour before (only if user opts in):  
“therapy at 2:30 — want a 20s ‘keys + water’ check now?”

store it as a separate micro-task so the pattern engine can learn which warm-ups boost attendance.

## 5) resonance tags, not just afterfeel
after “did,” offer one-tap **reason tags** (up to 2): `light`, `sound`, `movement`, `water`, `social`, `novelty`.  
this teaches the engine what *kind* of stimulus helps you in each container.

# pattern engine upgrades

## 6) container-aware insights (non-judgmental, specific)
- “**Afternoon is friction**: attendance 38% vs 82% morning. try moving scheduled to AM for 2 weeks?”
- “**Water cues help**: tasks with water had 2× completion in Evening.”
- “**Not relevant spikes on travel days**: auto-hide home-context tasks when location ≠ home?”

keep copy short, offer a one-tap experiment: *“try 2-week morning shift → yes/no”*.

## 7) streakless experiments
bundle changes into small experiments instead of settings:
- “**2-week experiment:** no afternoon appointments.”  
- “**1-week experiment:** evening only grounding = dim lights + water face.”

each experiment has a start/end and a *review* card. success = you keep it or discard it. no streaks, no shame.

## 8) missed-appointment taxonomy
your four outcomes are great; make **miss reasons** structured to unlock real insights:

```
miss_reason: 'logistics' | 'overwhelm' | 'conflict' | 'forgot' | 'transport' | 'cost' | 'not_ready'
```

then you can reflect: “misses = 60% logistics → add 5-min leave buffer?” (one-tap to auto-insert buffers before all PM appointments).

# UX micro-details (so it feels kind)

- **micro-copy:** “postponed with care” > “deferred”; “honor the no” > “skipped.”
- **animation:** on “did,” the card subtly *exhales* (scale 0.98, fade 120ms). on “skip,” it gently slides right and tucks under “later,” not disappearing (no shame).
- **haptics:** soft tap on “did,” none on “skip/forgot.”
- **icons:** grounding = 🌬️, my things = ✶, scheduled = ◷. glyphs beat heavy labels.

# notifications (PDA-safe variants)

- container open: “**Afternoon is here** — one soft option is waiting.”  
- appointment warmup: “**2:30 therapy later.** want a 20-second readiness check now?”  
- after miss (same day, once): “want to mark what happened so tomorrow fits better?” [do it] [not today]

# privacy + autonomy (non-negotiables)

- offline-first; no sign-in required; JSON export anytime.  
- no streaks or public leaderboards.  
- “**Silent day**” master toggle: mutes everything until tomorrow.

## The Refined Architecture

### Five Tracking Outcomes (Not Four)
```
[did it] [skipped] [forgot] [couldn't] [not relevant]
```

**"Not relevant"** is critical. It means: "This showed up but context changed" (travel, guests, life happened). Doesn't pollute your pattern data.

---

### Four Time Containers (With Smart Defaults)

**1. Late Night / Early Morning** (12am-6am)
- Breath work
- Body scan
- Gentle stretches
- Water sip
- Dim one light

**2. Morning** (6am-12pm)
- Water face
- Open window
- Sip water
- One hygiene anchor
- Medication check

**3. Afternoon** (12pm-6pm)
- Stand + sigh
- Notice 3 blues
- Stretch neck
- Phone screen wipe
- Hydration check

**4. Evening** (6pm-12am)
- Dim lights
- Change to soft clothes
- Lie on floor 2min
- Journal 2 sentences
- Evening medication

**You can add/remove/customize any of these.**

---

## First Pixel Win (Anti-Paralysis)

When you open a container, you see **ONE card first**:

```
┌─────────────────────────────────────┐
│ Afternoon                           │
│                                     │
│ Want to try one tiny thing?         │
│                                     │
│ ┌───────────────────────────────┐   │
│ │ Stand + sigh twice (10s)      │   │
│ │ big inhale, longer exhale     │   │
│ │                               │   │
│ │ [do it] [skip] [show all]     │   │
│ └───────────────────────────────┘   │
│                                     │
└─────────────────────────────────────┘
```

One action. If you do it → momentum. If you skip → no shame, everything else appears.

---

## Pressure Governor (Auto-Softens)

If you mark multiple "forgot" or "couldn't" in a container, next time it opens:

```
Noticed afternoon's been hard.
Want to try lighter mode?

[yes, soften] [no, keep normal]
```

**Lighter mode:**
- Shows only 2 items (one grounding, one custom)
- Feather-tone language ("peek?" "tiny try?")
- No notifications for that container
- Big **"Zero-Demand Day"** button at top

---

## Appointment Warm-Ups (Game-Changer)

When you add an appointment, you can enable a **pre-ritual**:

```
┌─────────────────────────────────────┐
│ Add Appointment                     │
│                                     │
│ What: Therapy                       │
│ Time: 2:30pm                        │
│ Date: Oct 22                        │
│                                     │
│ ☐ Warm-up ritual (recommended)     │
│   1 hour before: "keys + water      │
│   check" (20s)                      │
│                                     │
│ ☐ Travel buffer (5min early alert) │
│                                     │
│ [add appointment]                   │
└─────────────────────────────────────┘
```

Then at 1:30pm, you get:

```
Therapy at 2:30 later.
Want a 20-second readiness check now?

[keys + water check] [not yet] [silent until 2pm]
```

The app tracks: "warm-ups → 80% attendance vs 40% without."

---

## Resonance Tags (Why It Worked)

After marking "did it," one more tap:

```
water face ✓

What helped?
[water] [light] [movement] [sound] [novelty] [social]

(pick up to 2, or skip)
```

Then the pattern engine learns:
- "Water cues help you in Evening container"
- "Movement tags = 2× completion in Morning"
- "Sound-paired tasks work better on weekends"

---

Weekly view shows:

```
┌─────────────────────────────────────┐

│ WHAT'S WORKING                      │

│                                     │

```

**Experiments are one-tap:**

```
[forgot] [overwhelm] [transport] [conflict] 
[logistics] [cost] [wasn't ready]

[mark] [skip this]
```

## The Full Data Model

```typescript
interface TimeContainer {
  id: 'late' | 'morning' | 'afternoon' | 'evening';
  label: string;
  window: [number, number];
  pressure_mode: 'normal' | 'softened';
  items: ContainerItem[];
}

interface ContainerItem {
  id: string;
  type: 'grounding' | 'custom' | 'appointment';
  title: string;
  body_cue?: string;
  micro_version?: string;

// If appointment
  time?: string;
  date?: string;
  warmup?: {
    enabled: boolean;
    task: string;
    offset_minutes: number;
  };
  buffer?: {
    minutes: number;
    before: boolean;
  };
}

interface Completion {
  item_id: string;
  container: string;
  date: string;
  outcome: 'did' | 'skipped' | 'forgot' | 'couldnt' | 'not_relevant';

// If did
  afterfeel?: 'lighter' | 'same' | 'spikier';
  resonance_tags?: ('water' | 'light' | 'movement' | 'sound' | 'novelty' | 'social')[];

// If appointment missed
  miss_reason?: 'forgot' | 'overwhelm' | 'transport' | 'conflict' | 'logistics' | 'cost' | 'not_ready';
}

interface PatternInsight {
  type: 'resonating' | 'not_sticking' | 'container_pattern' | 'appointment_pattern' | 'tag_pattern';
  item_id?: string;
  container_id?: string;
  metric: number;
  message: string;
  action?: {
    label: string;
    experiment_id?: string;
  };
}

interface Experiment {
  id: string;
  title: string;
  duration_days: number;
  start_date: string;
  end_date: string;
  changes: string[];  // What you're testing
  status: 'active' | 'completed' | 'abandoned';
  result?: string;    // What you learned
}
```

---

## Build Priority (Tight Sequence)

### Phase 1: Core Loop
1. Four containers with grounding defaults

3. Five-outcome tracking (did/skip/forgot/couldn't/not relevant)
4. First pixel win (one card shown first)

### Phase 2: Appointments

### Phase 3: Intelligence

11. Pressure governor (auto-soften)
12. Experiment cards (2-week tests)

---

## Micro-Copy Bank (Drop-In)

**Outcomes:**
- Did: "good contact" / "system listened" / "thread tugged"
- Skip: "honor the no" / "rest coin earned" / "postponed with care"
- Forgot: "it happens" / "no harm" / "noted"
- Couldn't: "context shifted" / "makes sense" / "marked"
- Not relevant: "context changed" / "not today's shape" / "noted"

**Invitations:**
- Feather: "peek?" / "tiny try?" / "touch this for 20s?"
- Neutral: "want to try?" / "ready for this?" / "one attempt?"
- Crisp: "do the thing?" / "ready?" / "go time?"

**Notifications:**
- Container: "Afternoon is here — one soft option waiting"
- Warmup: "Therapy at 2:30 later. 20s readiness check now?"
- Miss follow-up: "Want to mark what happened so tomorrow fits better?"

---

**Not a calendar. Not a task list.**

**A compassionate mirror that:**
- Shows you your natural rhythms
- Tracks what actually works
- Softens when you're struggling
- Helps you see patterns without shame
- Treats appointments and tasks equally
- Learns which contexts help you

**It's a nervous system interface, not a productivity tool.**

### 🌬 small but potent refinements
**1. time container overlap**  
let them blur—people’s real rhythms drift. let the app notice when a task migrates across containers (“water face happened in afternoon 4× this week”) and *adapt* instead of correcting.

**2. resonance graph**

let insights fade if they stop repeating. patterns shouldn’t become prophecies. store the last 30 days as high-weight, older as low-weight so the mirror always reflects the *current nervous system.*

keeps metrics tethered to embodied truth.

[1761103688.906357] Assistant: ahh, I see it. Two likely culprits:

1) a full-screen overlay is quietly stealing touches (your **phraseOverlay**), and  
2) the **pointerEvents="box-none"** on the outcome button wrapper can behave weirdly on Android inside scroll views.

Here’s a surgical fix set:

---

### 1) Let taps pass through the phrase overlay
Right now, whenever `completionPhrase` has any value, this view sits on top of the screen and absorbs touches.

**Change:**
```diff
- <View style={styles.phraseOverlay}>
+ <View style={[styles.phraseOverlay, { pointerEvents: 'none' }]}>
```

That makes it display without blocking input.

---

### 2) Remove pointerEvents from the outcome buttons wrapper
This can suppress child touchables on some devices.

**Change in Item Detail screen:**
```diff
- <View style={styles.outcomeButtons} pointerEvents="box-none">
+ <View style={styles.outcomeButtons}>
```

---

### 3) (Sanity) Ensure the Done button isn’t just disabled
On the **outcome** screen, `done` is disabled until an afterfeel is tapped:
```tsx
disabled={!tempAfterfeel}
style={[styles.doneButton, { opacity: tempAfterfeel ? 1 : 0.5 }]}
```
So: you must tap **lighter / same / spikier** first. If you already are and it’s still not firing, #1 or #2 was the blocker.

---

### 4) Optional safety: only mount overlays/modals when truly needed

---

You did most of this already, but if you want to be extra sure no ghost overlay is up, add guards:

- `completionPhrase && <View ... pointerEvents="none" />`
- `showShiftModal && <Modal ... />`
- `addModalVisible && <Modal ... />`

(You already do that; the `pointerEvents` tweak is the key.)

---

### 5) Quick debug hooks (remove later)
If you still get stuck, drop these temporarily:

```tsx
<TouchableOpacity
  onPress={() => { console.log('did it pressed'); handleOutcomeSelect('did'); }}
  ...
/>

<TouchableOpacity
  onPress={() => { console.log('done pressed', { tempAfterfeel, tempTags }); 
                   if (tempAfterfeel) handleAfterfeelSelect(tempAfterfeel, tempTags); }}
  ...
/>
```

If logs fire, UI is fine and state logic is gating. If logs don’t fire, an overlay is still intercepting.

---

### Tiny polish while we’re in there
Make the exhale feel organic every time (you already randomized amplitude—nice). To avoid stuck animations, add a stop before starting:

```tsx
pulseAnim.stopAnimation(() => {
  pulseAnim.setValue(1);
  Animated.sequence([...]).start();
});
```

---

Try the two main changes (#1 and #2) first. 9/10 that unfreezes both **did it** and **done**.

import React, { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import {

} from 'react-native';

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// TYPE DEFINITIONS
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

interface TaskCard {
  id: string;
  title: string;
  modes: string[];
  context: string[];
  states_fit: string[];
  demand_level: number;
  body_cue: string;
  narratives: string[];

}

}

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

const TASK_DECK: TaskCard[] = [

  },
  {

    title: 'wipe phone screen 20s',
    modes: ['20s'],
    context: ['anywhere', 'phone-only'],
    states_fit: ['foggy', 'buzzy', 'steady'],
    demand_level: 1,

    next_suggestions: ['check one message', 'stretch wrists']
  },
  {
    id: 'stand-sigh',
    title: 'stand + sigh twice',
    modes: ['10s'],
    context: ['anywhere'],
    states_fit: ['frazzled', 'buzzy', 'foggy'],
    demand_level: 1,
    body_cue: 'big inhale, longer exhale with sound',
    narratives: ['release', 'body'],
    offer_text: ['want to let something go?', 'two sighs?'],

  },

    narratives: ['reset', 'body'],

  }
];

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// THEME PALETTES
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

const THEMES: { [key: string]: ThemeColors } = {
  forest: {
    bg: '#1a2f23',
    card: '#2d4a3a',
    accent: '#6b9e78',
    text: '#e8f4ea',
    dim: '#5a7a64'
  },
  studio: {
    bg: '#f5f5f0',
    card: '#ffffff',
    accent: '#4a5568',

---

text: '#2d3748',
    dim: '#a0aec0'
  },
  cosmic: {
    bg: '#0f0f23',
    card: '#1a1a3e',
    accent: '#7c3aed',
    text: '#e9d5ff',
    dim: '#6b46c1'
  }
};

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// MAIN COMPONENT
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

export default function HomeScreen() {
  const [theme, setTheme] = useState('forest');
  const [currentScreen, setCurrentScreen] = useState('state-select');
  const [selectedState, setSelectedState] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [currentOffers, setCurrentOffers] = useState<TaskCard[]>([]);
  const [activeCard, setActiveCard] = useState<TaskCard | null>(null);
  const [lastTaskId, setLastTaskId] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [fadeAnim] = useState(new Animated.Value(0));

const colors = THEMES[theme];

// Animate screen transitions
  useEffect(() => {
    Animated.timing(fadeAnim, {
      toValue: 1,
      duration: 400,
      useNativeDriver: true
    }).start();
  }, [currentScreen]);

// Filter and shuffle offers based on state
  const generateOffers = (state: string): TaskCard[] => {
    const filtered = TASK_DECK.filter(task => 
      task.states_fit.includes(state) && task.id !== lastTaskId
    );

// Weighted shuffle favoring low demand
    const weighted = filtered.map(task => ({
      task,
      weight: task.demand_level === 1 ? 2 : 1
    }));

const shuffled: TaskCard[] = [];
    while (weighted.length > 0 && shuffled.length < 3) {
      const totalWeight = weighted.reduce((sum, w) => sum + w.weight, 0);
      let random = Math.random() * totalWeight;

for (let i = 0; i < weighted.length; i++) {
        random -= weighted[i].weight;
        if (random <= 0) {
          shuffled.push(weighted[i].task);
          weighted.splice(i, 1);
          break;
        }
      }
    }

return shuffled;
  };

// Handle state selection
  const handleStateSelect = (state: string) => {
    setSelectedState(state);
    const offers = generateOffers(state);
    setCurrentOffers(offers);
    setCurrentScreen('offers');
    fadeAnim.setValue(0);
  };

// Handle card open
  const handleCardOpen = (task: TaskCard) => {
    setActiveCard(task);
    setCurrentScreen('card-detail');
    fadeAnim.setValue(0);
  };

// Handle skip
  const handleSkip = () => {
    if (activeCard) {
      setLastTaskId(activeCard.id);
    }
    setActiveCard(null);
    setCurrentScreen('offers');
    fadeAnim.setValue(0);
  };

// Handle complete
  const handleComplete = () => {
    if (activeCard && selectedState) {
      setLastTaskId(activeCard.id);
      const nextOffers = generateOffers(selectedState);
      setCurrentOffers(nextOffers);
      setActiveCard(null);
      setCurrentScreen('offers');
      fadeAnim.setValue(0);
    }
  };

// Roll dice for random card
  const handleRollDice = () => {
    if (!selectedState) return;

const allValid = TASK_DECK.filter(task => 
      task.states_fit.includes(selectedState) && task.id !== lastTaskId
    );
    if (allValid.length > 0) {
      const random = allValid[Math.floor(Math.random() * allValid.length)];
      handleCardOpen(random);
    }
  };

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
  // RENDER: State Select Screen
  // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

if (currentScreen === 'state-select') {
    return (
      <View style={[styles.container, { backgroundColor: colors.bg }]}>
        <Animated.View style={[styles.content, { opacity: fadeAnim }]}>
          <Text style={[styles.mainTitle, { color: colors.text }]}>
            How do you feel?
          </Text>

<Text style={[styles.subtitle, { color: colors.dim }]}>
            just notice, no judgment
          </Text>

{/* State chips */}
          <View style={styles.stateChipsContainer}>
            <TouchableOpacity
              onPress={() => handleStateSelect('foggy')}
              style={[styles.stateChip, { backgroundColor: colors.card, borderColor: colors.accent }]}
            >
              <Text style={[styles.stateChipTitle, { color: colors.text }]}>
                foggy
              </Text>
              <Text style={[styles.stateChipDesc, { color: colors.dim }]}>
                low energy, soft focus, drifting
              </Text>
            </TouchableOpacity>

<TouchableOpacity
              onPress={() => handleStateSelect('buzzy')}
              style={[styles.stateChip, { backgroundColor: colors.card, borderColor: colors.accent }]}
            >
              <Text style={[styles.stateChipTitle, { color: colors.text }]}>
                buzzy
              </Text>
              <Text style={[styles.stateChipDesc, { color: colors.dim }]}>
                frazzled, spiky, overstimulated
              </Text>
            </TouchableOpacity>

<TouchableOpacity
              onPress={() => handleStateSelect('steady')}
              style={[styles.stateChip, { backgroundColor: colors.card, borderColor: colors.accent }]}
            >

[1761104000.723] User: tried changing those two things, still doing it
[1761104042.20209] Assistant: Got you. If those two tweaks didn’t free it, this is almost certainly a **touch-capture** issue on Android: something full-screen and transparent is still sitting “above” your buttons. Two fixes tend to resolve this 100% of the time.

---

---

## 0) Quick confirm
Before changing code, do this sanity check:

- Temporarily **comment out** the whole `/* Completion phrase overlay */` block **and** the `/* Shift modal */` block.
- Reload and try the buttons.  
If they work, it’s definitely an overlay/z-order thing (most likely the phrase overlay).

---

## 1) Make the phrase overlay *truly* non-blocking on Android
On some RN/Android combos, `pointerEvents: 'none'` is **not enough** if the view has an alpha background and sits at `zIndex` 0. Give it no hitbox + push it behind:

```diff
- {completionPhrase && (
-   <View style={styles.phraseOverlay}>
+ {completionPhrase && (
+   <View style={[styles.phraseOverlay, { pointerEvents: 'none', zIndex: -1, elevation: -1 }]}>
      <View style={[styles.phraseBox, { backgroundColor: colors.card }]}>
        <Text style={[styles.phraseText, { color: colors.accent }]}>
          {completionPhrase}
        </Text>
      </View>
    </View>
)}
```

And soften the overlay bg so even if it rendered above by mistake, it’s not intercepting:

```diff
phraseOverlay: {
  position: 'absolute',
  top: 0, left: 0, right: 0, bottom: 0,
  justifyContent: 'center',
  alignItems: 'center',
- backgroundColor: 'rgba(0,0,0,0.3)',
+ backgroundColor: 'transparent', // no alpha layer to catch touches
},
```

(You still get the phrase box; you just lose the dim curtain. If you want the dim, render it *inside the box* as an inner shadow, not as a full-screen layer.)

---

## 2) Make sure the outcome buttons are in a touchable layer
Sometimes nested ScrollViews + Animated wrappers make the hitSlop weird. Force this stack:

```diff
- <Animated.ScrollView ...>
+ <Animated.ScrollView ... contentContainerStyle={[styles.scrollContent, { paddingBottom: 80 }]}>
    ...
-   <View style={styles.outcomeButtons}>
+   <View style={[styles.outcomeButtons, { pointerEvents: 'auto', zIndex: 1 }]}>
      <TouchableOpacity
        activeOpacity={0.7}
        onPress={() => handleOutcomeSelect('did')}
        style={[styles.outcomeButton, { backgroundColor: colors.accent }]}
      >
        <Text style={[styles.outcomeButtonText, { color: colors.text }]}>did it</Text>
      </TouchableOpacity>
      ...
    </View>
  </Animated.ScrollView>
```

Also remove any lingering `pointerEvents="box-none"` anywhere near those buttons (you already did).

---

## 3) Guard against “invisible modal” cases
Very occasionally, a `Modal` on Android can remain mounted strangely if a parent state race happens. Add an explicit `visible` guard **and** unmount:

```tsx
{addModalVisible ? (
  <Modal visible transparent animationType="slide"> ... </Modal>
) : null}

{showShiftModal ? (
  <Modal visible transparent animationType="fade"> ... </Modal>
) : null}
```

(You’re close to this already; this forces unmount.)

---

## 4) Make the “Done” logic foolproof

---

You gate “done” by `tempAfterfeel`. Good. Add a tiny protection so the press *always* fires when enabled:

```diff
<TouchableOpacity
- onPress={() => { if (tempAfterfeel) handleAfterfeelSelect(tempAfterfeel, tempTags); }}
+ onPress={() => {
+   if (!tempAfterfeel) return;
+   requestAnimationFrame(() => handleAfterfeelSelect(tempAfterfeel!, tempTags));
+ }}
  style={[styles.doneButton, { backgroundColor: colors.accent, opacity: tempAfterfeel ? 1 : 0.5 }]}
  disabled={!tempAfterfeel}
>
```

`requestAnimationFrame` avoids a rare case where a simultaneous re-render + overlay mount swallows the tap on Android.

---

## 5) If it’s still stuck, add a touch sniffer (temporary)
Drop this near the root of each problem screen to see whether taps are reaching the layer:

```tsx
<View
  style={{ position:'absolute', left:0, right:0, bottom:0, height:1 }}
  pointerEvents="auto"
  onStartShouldSetResponder={() => { console.log('root touch seen'); return false; }}
/>
```

If that never logs, something is still intercepting (overlay or Modal). If it logs, your button’s `onPress` is gated by state.

---

## 6) One more Android quirk
If you’re testing on Android with **Developer Options → Remove animations** or “Show taps,” TouchableOpacity can look permanently highlighted. Ensure those are off while testing to avoid false signals.

---

### TL;DR sequence
1) Comment out phrase overlay & modals → test.  
2) If fixed, re-enable with **`pointerEvents:'none'` + `zIndex:-1` + `elevation:-1` + no full-screen alpha**.  
3) Force outcome button container to `pointerEvents:'auto'` and `zIndex:1`.  
4) Wrap `done` press in `requestAnimationFrame`.

Ping me with what changes the behavior; I’ll zero in from there.

## 2) five outcomes (did / skipped / forgot / couldn’t / not relevant)

---

You already have “done” & “skip”. Let’s add the rest on the detail screen, so your pattern engine can exist later.

```ts
type Outcome = 'did' | 'skipped' | 'forgot' | 'couldnt' | 'not_relevant';

interface Completion {
  task_id: string;
  date: string;
  state?: string;
  outcome: Outcome;
}

const [completions, setCompletions] = useState<Completion[]>([]);

const logOutcome = (outcome: Outcome) => {
  if (!activeCard) return;
  setCompletions(prev => [
    ...prev,
    { task_id: activeCard.id, date: new Date().toISOString(), state: selectedState ?? undefined, outcome }
  ]);
};
```

Wire buttons:
```tsx

    onPress={() => { logOutcome('did'); handleComplete(); }}

    </TouchableOpacity>

<TouchableOpacity
      onPress={() => { logOutcome('skipped'); handleSkip(); }}

    >

{/* new row for non-shame outcomes */}
  <View style={[styles.secondaryButtons, { marginTop: 8 }]}>

    </TouchableOpacity>
    <TouchableOpacity
      onPress={() => { logOutcome('couldnt'); handleSkip(); }}
      style={[styles.secondaryButton, { backgroundColor: colors.card, borderColor: colors.dim }]}
    >
      <Text style={[styles.secondaryButtonText, { color: colors.dim }]}>couldn’t</Text>
    </TouchableOpacity>
    <TouchableOpacity

</View>
```

## 3) “pressure governor” lite (auto-soften on rough runs)
We’ll infer pressure from recent outcomes and collapse UI when spiky.

```ts
const [softMode, setSoftMode] = useState(false);

useEffect(() => {
  // simple heuristic: if last 3 outcomes in this state are non-did, soften
  const recent = completions.slice(-5).filter(c => c.state === selectedState);
  const nonDid = recent.filter(c => c.outcome !== 'did').length;
  setSoftMode(nonDid >= 3);
}, [completions, selectedState]);

// In offers screen:
<Text style={[styles.offerPrompt, { color: colors.dim }]}>
  {softMode ? 'one soft option is waiting' : 'want to try one of these?'}
</Text>

// When soft, limit offers to 2 + feather copy
const visibleOffers = softMode ? currentOffers.slice(0, 2) : currentOffers;
```

(you can also silence notifications in `softMode` when you add them later.)

---

## 4) guard your generator (when no tasks match)
If the filter returns 0, you currently end up with empties. Add a fallback:

```ts
const generateOffers = (state: string): TaskCard[] => {
  let pool = TASK_DECK.filter(t => t.states_fit.includes(state) && t.id !== lastTaskId);
  if (pool.length === 0) pool = TASK_DECK.filter(t => t.id !== lastTaskId); // graceful fallback
  // …weighted shuffle as you have…
  return shuffled.length ? shuffled : pool.slice(0, 3);
};
```

---

## 5) persistence (two lines, huge payoff)
Keep state across app restarts with `@react-native-async-storage/async-storage`.

```bash
expo install @react-native-async-storage/async-storage
```

```ts
import AsyncStorage from '@react-native-async-storage/async-storage';

useEffect(() => {
  (async () => {
    const raw = await AsyncStorage.getItem('completions');
    if (raw) setCompletions(JSON.parse(raw));
  })();
}, []);

useEffect(() => {
  AsyncStorage.setItem('completions', JSON.stringify(completions));
}, [completions]);
```
